## String References

- **13922**: Oh no, you don't. I can't say squat until you enable that orrriginal objective in the "Objective List" under the "Records of Eminence" section of the Quests menu.
- **13923**: Congrrratulations! Oh, if only I were as lucky as you.
- **13924**: Here goes nothing. <Hffffff> You're the eleven thousand, one hundred eleventh person to ever visit this square! To honor this crrrowning achievement, we're prepared to offer you a hefty reward.
- **13925**: How will you respond? [How hefty?/None of this poppycock for me./Don't. Just walk away.]
- **13926**: Hey, wait up! It's not like I go around saying this to everyone who comes my way. You're trrruly the eleven thousand, one hundred eleventh visitor!
- **13927**: Poppycock? I'll tell you what's poppycock--thrrrowing away your chance to receive a breath-stopping prize for being the eleven thousand, one hundred eleventh visitor!
- **13928**: Ah, frrresh meat! Stay a while and listen!
- **13929**: They say that currriosity killed the cat, but I can assure you that's not the case here!
- **13930**: Now, to peel back the veritable curtain on your commemorative prrrize...
- **13931**: Ta-daaa, it's $6! Aren't you in rrrapture now?
- **13932**: This is no ordinary, run-of-the-mill doll, I'll have you know! It's imbued with sorcery and engrrraves your "Records of Eminence" exploits upon itself. Now that's a handy-dandy tool if I've ever heard of one!
- **13933**: Anything to say? [I've always wanted one of those!/Records of Eminence...?]
- **13934**: Exactly! You can never go wrrrong with them, I say. But wait... There's more!
- **13935**: What? You rrreally haven't heard? I thought everyone had heard.
- **13936**: It may come as a surprrrise to you, but you're already participating in Records of Eminence!
- **13937**: Yes, indeed. By talking to me, you've cleared your first objective. That means you eventually get another prrresent: "sparks of eminence," which act as quantifiers of your grandeur!
- **13938**: Anyway, now that you know the rrropes, it's time to try it out yourself.
- **13939**: Just sink your fangs into any of the short quests in the "Objective List" under the "Records of Eminence" part of the Quests menu.
- **13940**: Easier than scooping fish from a bowl, rrright?
- **13941**: Certain objectives can be rrrepeated too, so there'll be more to try than there are cats in a clowder.
- **13942**: Once you accrrrue enough sparks from completing objectives, come talk to me for a saucer of good stuff.
- **13943**: Uh-oh. I don't know how you managed to sneak this past me, but you're as weak as a kitten. Quit pussyfooting around and talk to Selele. She'll help you brrrush up on the basics...for free.
- **13944**: Speaking of good stuff, your $3 is top-notch, so I'm going to need $1 gil for it.
- **13945**: ...Not convinced it's worth the price?
- **13946**: Okay, then. Watch this.
- **13947**: With a simple clap of the hands, it disappears like magic! That's the power of arcane blah-ditty-blah at its finest!
- **13948**: So, yeah, there's no way I could entrrrust something so amazing to you for free. Pay up.
- **13949**: Pay $1 gil? [I've only got catcalls for you./Go cat around somewhere else./I feel like a bag of cats, so no.]
- **13950**: ...Fine, then.
- **13951**: Huh? Why am I giving up so easily?
- **13952**: Because the doll's already rrregistered to you. If you're not going to give me money, there's nothing I can do about it.
- **13959**: The $3 nods in agreement.
- **13962**: You wish to exchange your sparks?
- **13963**: Wait but a moment. Here, follow this command--er, compendium to the letter and you should be able to learn a thing or two.
- **13964**: Ah, this almost slipped my mind. Should you receive any distinct sorts of artifacts for completing an objective, I will be happy to take them off your hands.
- **13965**: Worry not, for I am not out to rob you of your glory. Quite the opposite, in fact. I will provide you with a worthwhile reward for collecting them.
- **13966**: Thanks to your donation, I now have $1 $0 in safekeeping.
- **13967**: Ohohoho... Turning in sparks, I see.
- **13968**: Hold your hoarder hares, [hero/heroine]. I love your enthusiasm, but you're not going to learn a thing without a primer. Ahhh, here it is. Have a look-see.
- **13969**: Oh, crud, that's right. You should trade me any special artifacts you get from completing objectives.
- **13970**: "Why?" What a good question! Because I'll give you a nifty-difty reward at some point, that's why.
- **13971**: Thanks for doing what I asked. Now I have a total of $1 $0 from you.
- **13972**: You want to trrrade in sparks, do you?
- **13973**: Let me wake up from my catnap first... Alright, now rrread this.
- **13974**: Wait, wait, wait! If you happen to get any purrrticularly interesting items from completing objectives, send them my way.
- **13975**: Don't worry, I'm as honest as a cat when the meat's out of reach. I thought of taking them all for myself...
- **13976**: ...but I don't want to make fur fly with the Adventurers' Mutual Aid Network, so I'll do my job like a good little kitty.
- **13977**: Grrreat! Now I have $1 $0 from you. Thanks!
- **13978**: Hm? Oh, spark exchange... Of course.
- **13979**: I'm not completely sure, but this might help set some things straight...I guess.
- **13980**: Let me add one thing. Please find it within your heart of hearts to give me anything of particular interest you get from completing objectives.
- **13981**: I'll make it worth your while...I swear! Just keep at it and I'll be sure to surprise you with a hot, steaming helping of gratitude one day.
- **13982**: Thanks for doing your part in the battle for love and peace! With this latest addition, I'm now keeping track of $1 $0 for you.
- **13983**: "Records of Eminence is a system brought to you by the caring folks at the Adventurers' Mutual Aid Network. Set your own objectives whenever you desire, and fulfill them to receive compensation.
- **13984**: "To set these tasks, select 'Objective List' from the Quests menu, then choose ones that suit your fancy.
- **13985**: "You will be rewarded with gil and sparks of eminence, which serve the same function as points, notes, credits, and other such methods of quantifying one's greatness. As an extra incentive, adventurers will earn three times the amount of sparks for completing an objective the first time.
- **13986**: "Completed objectives will vanish from your quest list unless they are repeatable. In the event that you wish to make room for different objectives, you will need to remove them manually.
- **13987**: "As a corollary regarding rewards, you'll be blessed with special remuneration once you complete several objectives. Bask in the joy of these pleasant surprises when you can.
- **13988**: "Lastly, any and all acts of vandalism directed against your $3 are strictly prohibited.
- **13989**: "Should you be found in violation of this dictum, the heavens will part and wrath incarnate will descend to mete out justice upon you."

126: 0x0E4A [0x48] [System] [13983*]:
    → ""Records of Eminence is a system brought to you by the caring folks at the Adventurers' Mutual Aid Network. Set your own objectives whenever you desire, and fulfill them to receive compensation."

128: 0x0E4E [0x48] [System] [13984*]:
    → ""To set these tasks, select 'Objective List' from the Quests menu, then choose ones that suit your fancy."
129: 0x0E51 [0x23] WAIT_FOR_DIALOG_INTERACTION
130: 0x0E52 [0x48] [System] [13985*]:
    → ""You will be rewarded with gil and sparks of eminence, which serve the same function as points, notes, credits, and other such methods of quantifying one's greatness. As an extra incentive, adventurers will earn three times the amount of sparks for completing an objective the first time."
131: 0x0E55 [0x23] WAIT_FOR_DIALOG_INTERACTION
132: 0x0E56 [0x48] [System] [13986*]:
    → ""Completed objectives will vanish from your quest list unless they are repeatable. In the event that you wish to make room for different objectives, you will need to remove them manually."

134: 0x0E5A [0x48] [System] [13987*]:
